user
You are the L2 research decision reviewer for this project. Give a clear decision, not a hedge.

PROJECT: IEEE TIM paper on conformal RUL intervals for C-MAPSS turbofan engines.

METHOD: end-to-end conditional quantile regression + CQR calibration. Backbone trained on pinball(0.05)+pinball(0.95)+0
.1*MSE. Half-width head s=softplus(g(z))>=0, interval [c-s, c+s]. Then CQR: q_hat = Quantile(E_i), interval [q_lo-q_hat
, q_hi+q_hat].

REAL RESULTS (all actually run):
- 4 subsets (FD001-FD004) x 5 folds x 2 seeds = 40 cells, plus 2 more seeds (79/80 done)
- Nominal level (delta=0.1): CQR vs same-training constant-width baseline, 19/20 folds won, median -8.7%
- Matched coverage 0.90: CQR vs constant width 29/40 (72%) won, median -8.4%
- Per subset at matched coverage: FD001 -8.7%, FD002 -14.4%, FD003 -1.6%, FD004 -0.8%
- Cross-domain FEMTO bearings (6 bearings): CQR vs constant width 5/6 won, mean -12.2%
- Per-engine minimum coverage 0.50-0.71 while marginal coverage is 0.90
- A4 ablation permuting prior sigma_T: +0.2% mean over 20 folds (no effect)

ALREADY TRIED AND FAILED (real experiments):
- Asymmetric calibration (separate lower/upper quantiles): 9 ratios swept, optimum is exactly symmetric, no gain
- Stratified/Mondrian calibration by predicted RUL and by sigma_T (3/5/10 bins): all worse than global (+2% to +18%)
- Engine-level calibration (guarantee 90% of engines reach target): equivalent to simply widening the interval at match
ed marginal coverage (0.768 vs 0.770)
- Seed ensembling: n=2 helps (-6.0% -> -8.3%), n=4 degrades (-6.4%)
- Localized CQR (kernel on learned half-width): no gain on 40 cells, 11.7% worse on FD003

DIAGNOSTIC: the width head is working (coefficient of variation 0.33-0.44, correlation with |error| 0.25-0.42 on all su
bsets). FD003 has HIGHER correlation (0.352) than FD001 (0.253) yet almost no gain. Hypothesis: width variation is too 
small relative to the conformal quantile q_hat, which washes it out.

CONSTRAINT: the user refuses to publish negative results and wants continued optimization for a positive contribution.

DECISION NEEDED:
A. Change the training loss to a normalized pinball (Laplace NLL form): L = rho_0.05(q_lo,y)/s + rho_0.95(q_hi,y)/s + l
ambda*log(s). Mechanism: force s to be proportional to residual scale. Cost: retrain 40 cells x 1 seed ~2 hours. Uncert
ain payoff on FD003/FD004.
B. Accept the current scope: FD001/FD002 clear gains, FD003/FD004 ties; overall 29/40, median -8.4%, cross-domain 5/6. 
Write the subset dependence as scope of applicability.
C. Something else you think is better.

FILE: docs/FINAL_PROTOCOL.md
# STG-MambaNet Final Protocol

Status: P0 draft, frozen after P2  
Date: 2026-10-20  
Owner: Methodologist (Gemini decision), implementation by DeepSeek

This document is the single source of truth for data, model selection, loss
selection, ablation, and final test reporting. If code and this document
disagree, this document wins and the code must be changed.

## 1. Label protocol

- Main protocol: clip125 = True.
- Training targets: min(125, last_cycle - cycle).
- Validation targets: same piecewise-linear cap of 125.
- Official test targets: min(125, RUL_FDxxx.txt).
- Appendix only: raw (unclipped) test metrics must also be stored under
  appendix_raw in metrics.json and in the final appendix table.
- Main tables use clip125 only. Raw numbers must never be mixed into the main
  table or used for model selection.

## 2. Single production codebase

- The only legal production code is src/dataset.py, src/model.py, src/loss.py,
  scripts/run_ablation.py, scripts/summarize_ablations.py.
- The former root-level duplicates model.py, loss.py, dataset.py,
  sanity_check.py are archived under archive_root_v0/ and must not be imported.
- The archived root implementation is a reference for trajectory/monotonic
  logic only; all future changes happen in src/.

## 3. Test hold-out policy

- The 12 runs with seeds 42, 67, 123 are archived as Development Pilot.
- During P1 and P2, the official test set must not be read, printed, scored, or
  referenced. Model selection, early stopping, and hyperparameter search use
  only engine-level validation splits (20% of training engines) and the
  validation metrics val_rmse_clip125 / val_nasa_clip125.
- P1/P2 scripts must not instantiate a test DataLoader.
- After P1/P2 freeze, P3 uses exactly five pre-registered seeds:
  101, 202, 303, 404, 505.
- The official test set is evaluated once per final run, after loading the
  validation-selected checkpoint. No further model changes are allowed after
  the first P3 test result.

## 4. Fair ablation design

### 4.1 Parameter matching

- The w/o SSM LSTM control must be within +/-3% of the Full Model parameter
  count. The matched hidden width and any output projection must be recorded
  in config.json.
- Parameter counts are checked automatically before training.

### 4.2 Graph topology controls

AdaptiveSpatialBranch supports graph_mode:

- learnable: A = (1 - alpha) I + alpha * softmax(ReLU(E1 E2^T)),
  alpha initialized to 0.1 and learned.
- identity: A = I.
- random_fixed: a fixed, row-stochastic random matrix stored as a buffer.

The identity-graph control distinguishes graph propagation from extra
parameters. The random-fixed control checks whether any fixed mixing is
sufficient.

### 4.3 Final P3 variants

1. Full Model: learnable graph + SSM + full physics loss.
2. w/o A-GCN: independent per-sensor branch + SSM + full physics loss.
3. Identity-Graph: A = I + SSM + full physics loss.
4. w/o SSM: learnable graph + parameter-matched LSTM + full physics loss.
5. w/o Physics Loss: learnable graph + SSM + pure MSE.
6. w/o Mono Loss: learnable graph + SSM + MSE + asymmetric term only.

## 5. Metrics and gates

Main metrics:

- RMSE (clip125)
- NASA asymmetric score (clip125)
- MAE
- Mean signed error (positive = late prediction)
- Late_Fraction: fraction of samples with y_pred > y_true
- Severe_Late_Count: number of samples with y_pred - y_true > 10
- Mono_Violation_Rate: fraction of valid adjacent trajectory pairs with
  y_hat_t > y_hat_{t-1}

Appendix metrics:

- RMSE and NASA on raw (unclipped) test targets.

No hard-coded target such as "force test RMSE <= 13.0" is allowed. The
validation objective is to minimize mean val_rmse_clip125 and reduce seed
variance. Final results must report all five seeds, mean, standard deviation,
and paired deltas versus Full. No seed may be dropped after seeing test.

## 6. Physics loss protocol

The production loss supports four pre-registered variants:

1. Loss-MSE: pure MSE.
2. Loss-Asym: MSE + asymmetric late-penalty.
3. Loss-Mono: MSE + trajectory monotonic penalty.
4. Loss-Full: MSE + asymmetric + monotonic.

The total objective is:

L = MSE + lambda_asym * L_Asym + lambda_mono * L_Mono

with L_Asym using weight 2.0 for late errors and 1.0 for early errors.
gamma/late_weight, lambda_asym, and lambda_mono are selected on validation
only. The trajectory for L_Mono is the per-cycle RUL trajectory returned by
return_trajectory=True. If no trajectory is supplied, L_Mono is exactly zero
and monotonic_available is recorded as 0.

## 7. Multi-condition extension

FD001 remains the primary development set. After FD001 is frozen and if
compute allows, FD002 is an external validity check only. FD002 preprocessing
must use condition-aware normalization (K-Means operating-condition clusters,
per-cluster sensor statistics fitted on training engines only). FD002 must not
be used to retroactively change the frozen FD001 design.

## 8. Reproducibility and audit

Every run stores:

- config.json (all hyperparameters, graph_mode, loss flags, seed)
- split_seed_<seed>.json (train/validation/test engine ids)
- scaler_stats.json (training-only statistics)
- history.csv (per-epoch validation metrics)
- best_model.pt (validation-selected checkpoint)
- test_predictions.csv (per-engine y_true, y_pred, signed error)
- metrics.json (main clip125, appendix_raw, safety diagnostics)

Every final table must be generated from metrics.json / predictions.csv by
scripts/summarize_ablations.py. Manual numbers are forbidden.

## 9. Freeze record

P1 (validation-only) selected:

- window_size = 50
- dropout = 0.2
- weight_decay = 1e-4
- d_model = 64, batch_size = 256, lr = 1e-3, grad_clip = 1.0
- epochs = 80, patience = 20
- graph_mode = learnable, identity_prior_alpha = 0.1
- temporal = SSM, kernels = 3/5/7
- validation split = 80/20 engine-level, seeds 42/67/123
- Compute-limited execution: P1/P2 were completed as a seed-42 screen; the
  five-seed P3 is the confirmatory evidence. Recorded as a limitation.

P2 (validation-only) selected:

FILE: docs/FINAL_PROTOCOL.md
# STG-MambaNet Final Protocol

Status: P0 draft, frozen after P2  
Date: 2026-10-20  
Owner: Methodologist (Gemini decision), implementation by DeepSeek

This document is the single source of truth for data, model selection, loss
selection, ablation, and final test reporting. If code and this document
disagree, this document wins and the code must be changed.

## 1. Label protocol

- Main protocol: clip125 = True.
- Training targets: min(125, last_cycle - cycle).
- Validation targets: same piecewise-linear cap of 125.
- Official test targets: min(125, RUL_FDxxx.txt).
- Appendix only: raw (unclipped) test metrics must also be stored under
  appendix_raw in metrics.json and in the final appendix table.
- Main tables use clip125 only. Raw numbers must never be mixed into the main
  table or used for model selection.

## 2. Single production codebase

- The only legal production code is src/dataset.py, src/model.py, src/loss.py,
  scripts/run_ablation.py, scripts/summarize_ablations.py.
- The former root-level duplicates model.py, loss.py, dataset.py,
  sanity_check.py are archived under archive_root_v0/ and must not be imported.
- The archived root implementation is a reference for trajectory/monotonic
  logic only; all future changes happen in src/.

## 3. Test hold-out policy

- The 12 runs with seeds 42, 67, 123 are archived as Development Pilot.
- During P1 and P2, the official test set must not be read, printed, scored, or
  referenced. Model selection, early stopping, and hyperparameter search use
  only engine-level validation splits (20% of training engines) and the
  validation metrics val_rmse_clip125 / val_nasa_clip125.
- P1/P2 scripts must not instantiate a test DataLoader.
- After P1/P2 freeze, P3 uses exactly five pre-registered seeds:
  101, 202, 303, 404, 505.
- The official test set is evaluated once per final run, after loading the
  validation-selected checkpoint. No further model changes are allowed after
  the first P3 test result.

## 4. Fair ablation design

### 4.1 Parameter matching

- The w/o SSM LSTM control must be within +/-3% of the Full Model parameter
  count. The matched hidden width and any output projection must be recorded
  in config.json.
- Parameter counts are checked automatically before training.

### 4.2 Graph topology controls

AdaptiveSpatialBranch supports graph_mode:

- learnable: A = (1 - alpha) I + alpha * softmax(ReLU(E1 E2^T)),
  alpha initialized to 0.1 and learned.
- identity: A = I.
- random_fixed: a fixed, row-stochastic random matrix stored as a buffer.

The identity-graph control distinguishes graph propagation from extra
parameters. The random-fixed control checks whether any fixed mixing is
sufficient.

### 4.3 Final P3 variants

1. Full Model: learnable graph + SSM + full physics loss.
2. w/o A-GCN: independent per-sensor branch + SSM + full physics loss.
3. Identity-Graph: A = I + SSM + full physics loss.
4. w/o SSM: learnable graph + parameter-matched LSTM + full physics loss.
5. w/o Physics Loss: learnable graph + SSM + pure MSE.
6. w/o Mono Loss: learnable graph + SSM + MSE + asymmetric term only.

## 5. Metrics and gates

Main metrics:

- RMSE (clip125)
- NASA asymmetric score (clip125)
- MAE

DECISION: C — Run a bounded, validation-only width-rescaling experiment on existing checkpoints before changing the tra
ining loss.

REASONING: The washout hypothesis is plausible, but correlation with absolute error does not establish that the head id
entifies the conditional 90% error tail that determines interval efficiency. For fixed predictions, calibrated half-wid
th is \(s(x)+\hat q\), so additive calibration reduces relative width variation without changing its absolute variation
 or ranking. Testing \(a\,s(x)+\hat q_a\) directly probes whether insufficient width contrast causes the FD003/FD004 ti
es, without retraining. Reject A as written: with the same \(s\) defining both endpoints and the denominator, its loss 
simplifies to \(0.1+(|y-c|/s-1)_++\lambda\log s\), which changes the quantile-estimation objective and permits scale co
llapse on exactly fitted observations. Existing gains justify retaining the current method as the reference, but establ
ished [CQR](https://arxiv.org/abs/1905.03222) plus an ineffective prior does not establish a new methodological contrib
ution.

COUNTER-EVIDENCE: If validation-selected width amplification produces no reproducible improvement on FD003/FD004, insuf
ficient width contrast is unlikely to be the actionable bottleneck. If a separately justified residual-scale training o
bjective improves those subsets on untouched evaluation engines while preserving FD001/FD002 performance, rejecting tra
ining changes would have been premature.

RISKS:

- Repeated optimization against the same evaluation folds creates selection bias; additional seeds do not provide new i
ndependent engines.
- Width rescaling is a diagnostic and optimization step, not sufficient methodological novelty by itself.
- Marginal coverage does not resolve the observed engine-level undercoverage; ties and failed ablations must remain in 
the evidence record.

NEXT ACTIONS:

1. Have DSH record this decision under D001 and finish the missing FD004 run. Preserve existing checkpoints, prediction
s, and results.
2. Freeze every backbone and center prediction. Test only \(a\in\{0,0.5,1,2,4\}\), with scores
   \[
   E_i(a)=|y_i-c_i|-a s_i,\qquad
   \hat q_a=E_{(\lceil(n_{\rm cal}+1)0.90\rceil)}(a).
   \]
   Return intervals with half-width \(\max\{0,a s(x)+\hat q_a\}\). Here \(a=0\) recovers the same-center constant-width
 baseline; \(a=1\) recovers current CQR.
3. Select \(a\) using engine-disjoint inner validation splits, with separate inner calibration and scoring engines. Kee
p final calibration engines and official test labels outside selection. On validation, report signed \(\hat q_a\), cali
brated width variation, coverage, mean width, and upper-tail error versus predicted-width bins.
4. Pre-register the promotion gate: at least 3% median paired width reduction versus current CQR on **each** of FD003 a
nd FD004, with validation coverage at least 0.90 and at most 1% width degradation on either FD001 or FD002. Apply ident
ical evaluation rules to all candidates.
5. If the gate passes, freeze the selection procedure and evaluate across all subsets and seeds on untouched evaluation
 engines. If it fails, close the width-amplification route and return for an L2 decision on a distinct training hypothe
sis; do not launch A’s 40-cell retraining automatically. Report all outcomes, and label evaluation on previously inspec
ted engines as exploratory.
